# P0 — полная статическая консолидация current review decisions

Навигация: [P0](P0.md) · [P1](P1.md) · [P2](P2.md) · [input inventory](P0.md#input-inventory) · [source plans](#original-plan-items).

## Методика и проверенный срез

Это консолидация **всех 189 входных Markdown**, а не исправление кода и не новый exhaustive audit всего repository. Все входные тексты прочитаны целиком (включая тематические отчёты, engine TASK_GROUPS, root SUMMARY/CRATE_PRIORITY и collapsed history): **84777 строк**, **7740601 Unicode characters**. История использована для происхождения, механизма и исходных планов; действующие решения — authoritative prefix до первого `<details>`. Исторические повторы вторично не подсчитываются. Прежние verdicts были гипотезами: проверены current implementation, callers, контракты, existing oracle assertions и registrations, а не только summary labels.

Входные обзоры привязаны к frozen source `e3765c935fc71655ee1ec0160cf180607935d89b`; HEAD при старте — `66c19fe452c16cb587c8a3b7863ae63b0c07e15a`. Здесь source evidence относится **к доступному изменённому working tree**, не выдаётся за тот frozen snapshot. Полностью прочитаны 820 обнаруженных source/contract/config/test текстов (280466 строк); frozen git objects использованы для remap line numbers и различения внешних diff. До запуска уже существовали 28 tracked modifications и один untracked history-overlay test; они не принадлежат этому обзору и оставлены нетронутыми. SequenceMatcher сопоставляет frozen references с фактически прочитанными current строками; номера в source links — current. Ссылки на исходные review anchors сохраняют frozen происхождение allegation, не подменяют current доказательство.

Срез не атомарный: последовательные чтения, normalized UTF-8/LF text fingerprints и повторная static consistency сверка. Промежуточная сверка не обнаружила изменения прочитанных 189 inputs или 820 source texts; post-write static check результат отдельно передаётся вместе с handoff; этот текст фиксирует срез до создания файлов. Это не commit/deployment/installed-toolchain snapshot и не гарантия отсутствия изменений в непрочитанных файлах.

**Ничего не запускалось:** компилятор, cargo, сборка, clippy, formatter, tests, benchmarks, проект или воспроизведения. Выполнялись только чтение, read-only git diff/show, получение primary source texts и обработка уже прочитанных данных в памяти. Другие агенты не запускались. Единственные записи в repository — новые `P0.md`, `P1.md`, `P2.md`. Наличие test файла, manifest registration, старого green label, same-helper roundtrip или bare `is_err` не считается выполненной или достаточной проверкой.

### Правила группировки и verdict

Исходный P — **приоритет plan item, не severity**. Сохранены все 423 явно P-размеченных пункта: P0=87, P1=183, P2=153. Ещё 24 `shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md` Plan 1–24 восстановлены по настоящим historical P headers: 1–4→P0, 5–15→P1, 16–24→P2. Итого **P0=91, P1=194, P2=162; 447** исходных plan decisions. ID не переименованы. Каждый plan table находится на собственном исходном P, даже когда его mechanism canon расположен в более срочном файле. Shared plan не означает shared cause: составные планы и 19 engine TASK bundles направлены к отдельным leaves. Same cause по guarantee/trigger/caller из нескольких линз/crates имеет одну карточку; происхождение и scoped verdict каждой строки сохранены отдельно. Mixed `refuted/open` в одном canon означает разные формулировки/границы, не автоматическое превращение опровергнутой строки в баг.

NEW и текущие claims без P привязаны к соответствующему существующему механизму/плану; иначе самостоятельная P-классификация явно объяснена. Только для таких неразмеченных пунктов: P0 — срочные подтверждённые integrity/security/availability transitions; P1 — другая корректность/конкурентность/контракты и существенные unverified boundaries; P2 — scoped costs/coverage/docs/style без установленного серьёзного runtime harm. Исторический P0 у style/perf/testing задачи не заменён на P2. Risk, reachability и status — отдельные поля.

`confirmed-open` — source-backed текущий механизм/обязанность в указанном scope; `partially-fixed` — конкретная часть снята, остаток указан; `fixed` — названный original mechanism source-fixed, не passing-tests/release certificate; `refuted` — есть положительное counter-evidence; `not-applicable` — intentional policy/ownership/legacy/API redesign либо calibration non-finding без нарушенного обязательства; `unverified` — названа недостающая premise, **не safe**. Costs — структурные выводы, не измеренные latency/RSS/throughput/численные выигрыши. Public-library, privileged embedding, authenticated wire, source-compilation permission и locally corrupted persisted state различаются.

### Exact dependency/contract дисциплина

Current `Cargo.lock`/manifest slice: **Wasmtime 48.0.5** (входные reports проверяли 46.0.2), **rustls 0.23.45** (в reports 0.23.37), declared Rust **1.95.0** (не установленная/исполненная версия). Проверены соответствующие changed-pin ветки `validate_sync_call`, imported async requirements, `func_wrap_async`, provider auto-selection; tagged Rust 1.95 source подтверждает Vec initialization, numeric Hash dispatch, PartialEq law, token prefix copying и unbuffered Condvar notification. Обновление pins само по себе не закрывает getter/reentry, alias или lifecycle defects. В процитированных входных paragraphs старые версии — frozen provenance; current conclusions используют эту перепроверку.

Checksum SHA-256 **54** доступных published `.crate` archives совпал с current lockfile; inspected exact member texts — **79**, без extraction на диск. Среди load-bearing pins: Fjall/lsm-tree 3.1.6, Tokio 1.49.0, scc 3.8.4, rmp-serde 1.3.1, serde/serde_core 1.0.228, indexmap 2.14.0, libc 0.2.186, bincode 1.3.3, peak_alloc 0.3.0. NAPI 3.10.5 source проверен против exact manifest pin через published source; Node исключён из workspace, отдельно resolved transitive/build/native artifact граф не сертифицирован. Доступный installed `@msgpack/msgpack` 3.1.3 разрешён binding range `^3.0.0`, но это не universal npm-resolution guarantee. Curl parser evidence условно относится к 8.10.1-compatible config/resolve grammar; установленный curl не запускался и не идентифицирован. AArch64 alignment premise для vld1_lane_u32 разрешена по exact 1.95 generated source (typed *ptr); остальные target stack margins, deep macro parse/render/drop limits, quantitative HashDoS/compile/perf и out-of-tree consumers не объявлены доказанными.

Reference guarantees: alpha no-in-place-upgrade policy [README:33](../../../../README.md)/[CHANGELOG:13](../../../../CHANGELOG.md); scoped [SECURITY](../../../../SECURITY.md); [house rules](../../../../CLAUDE.md); storage durability/atomicity [Store contract:211–285](../../../../crates/shamir-storage/src/types.rs); actual test selection [runner:126–181](../../../../scripts/test.sh). Строгие wire/spec/identity обещания сверены с protocol docs и exact sources, а не выведены из успешной симметричной serialization.

Guidance здесь оставлено только для конкретно названного механизма/qualified plan. Пустое recipe/oracle поле не заполняется общим шаблоном. Cargo.lock/manifest citations означают provenance, а не callable seam; test-source citation сама по себе не доказывает регистрацию, target selection или покрытие данного consumer. Конкретные assertions, caller/registry facts и ограничения остаются в source/trigger/counter sections и plan ledger.

## Арифметика этого уровня

В других уровнях дополнительно 2 new active xxs source mechanisms и 1 external source-fixed slice; totals input cards/rows ниже их не смешивают.


Canonical input decision cards: **152** = active/partial/unverified **132** + closed/calibration **20**. These are deduplicated mechanisms/guarantee obligations plus explicitly named non-findings, **не число уникальных runtime bugs**. All input cards across P = **1147**; дополнительная external source-fixed slice-карточка находится в P1 и counted separately.

| Cards | Total | confirmed-open | fixed | partially-fixed | refuted | unverified | not-applicable |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| P0 | 152 | 130 | 8 | 1 | 8 | 1 | 4 |

Input coverage ledger в этом файле: Claim **479**, Plan **91**, Observation **25**. Во всех трёх файлах суммарно **2635/447/94**, каждая source row имеет однозначный primary coverage section; card origins/redirects могут повторять ссылки, но не увеличивают арифметику.

### RV-BENCH-UTILS-126E5E6F — Reproducibility key excludes any generator/dataset-format version

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `low`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-bench-utils`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.4](shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md#review-5-4); [shamir-bench-utils/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 4](shamir-bench-utils/api-wire-protocol.md#review-4).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P1. Исходные пункты: [shamir-bench-utils P0.2 (P0)](shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2); [shamir-bench-utils P1.3 (P1)](shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** The artifact and generated header provide neither algorithm version nor source revision. This is a report-provenance gap relative to the release checklist, not proof that fixed-code reproducibility is broken. Recording a source SHA can satisfy provenance without mandating a new public format constant.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** This supports provenance follow-up, not a claim that current fixed-source determinism fails or that DATASET_FORMAT_VERSION is the only valid solution.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/vector_data.rs:158](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/vector_data.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/examples/vector_report.rs:332](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/examples/vector_report.rs); [docs/dev-artifacts/roadmap/VECTOR_PRODUCTION_EXECUTION.md:245](../../roadmap/VECTOR_PRODUCTION_EXECUTION.md); [crates/shamir-engine/examples/vector_report.rs:336](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/examples/vector_report.rs).

<a id="rv-bench-utils-e6177532"></a>

### RV-BENCH-UTILS-E6177532 — The tracking allocator always moves reallocations, changing benchmark work rather than merely adding counters

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-bench-utils`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-bench-utils P0.1 (P0)](shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Checksummed published peak_alloc 0.3.0 src/lib.rs:168–185 implements realloc as System.alloc(new_size), accounting addition, copy of min(old,new), System.dealloc(old), and accounting subtraction; it never delegates System.realloc. A successful resizing request therefore incurs a mandatory copy and temporary old-plus-new allocation watermark. Linked fixture-only timing benches inherit this implementation. This is a source-proven instrumentation behavior omitted from assigned claims, not measured slowdown or memory unsafety. Source: https://docs.rs/crate/peak_alloc/0.3.0/source/src/lib.rs.

- **Qualified direction:** Сделать allocator instrumentation явно opt-in для выбранного binary/measurement; обычные timing/fixture-only paths не должны незаметно менять allocator. Отчёт обязан различать allocator/layout watermark и RSS и сообщать, что peak_alloc 0.3.0 заменяет realloc на allocate-copy-deallocate. Для измерения исходного allocator требуется сохраняющее его realloc/metric semantics instrumentation, а не admission quotas.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [Cargo.lock:2396](../../../../Cargo.lock); [crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/peak_mem.rs:39](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/peak_mem.rs); [crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/peak_mem.rs:37](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/peak_mem.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/Cargo.toml:107](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/Cargo.toml); [crates/shamir-index/benches/create_index_streaming.rs:24](../../../../crates/shamir-index/benches/create_index_streaming.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/benches/vector_search.rs:54](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/benches/vector_search.rs).

- **Прочитанные original source line fragments (indent stripped; separator /):** [crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/peak_mem.rs:40](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/peak_mem.rs): <code>static PEAK_ALLOC: PeakAlloc = PeakAlloc; / pub fn setup() { / let _ = &amp;PEAK_ALLOC;</code>.

### RV-BENCH-UTILS-E1F3D036 — `peak_mem`'s global allocator is silently active for every bench/example/test binary of shamir-engine and shamir-index, contradicting the documented "off by default" contract

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped coverage/oracle. **Модули:** `shamir-bench-utils`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.1](shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md#review-3-1); [shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.1](shamir-bench-utils/SUMMARY.md#review-5-1); [shamir-bench-utils/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 1](shamir-bench-utils/api-wire-protocol.md#review-1); [shamir-bench-utils/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 4](shamir-bench-utils/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-4); [shamir-bench-utils/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 4](shamir-bench-utils/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-4); [shamir-bench-utils/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-bench-utils/security-crypto.md#review-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Both dev-dependency entries enable peak_mem. filtered_vector_search and sq8_hot_path import vector fixtures, linking the helper allocator without setup or memory measurement. This contradicts ordinary-bench isolation, not the narrower fact that the helper package has no default feature.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Current edges are development-only; no ordinary database-production or secret-handling path is established. Fixture-only benches actually import the helper and inherit its allocator without setup. The allocator is declared in the library and enabled through current dev dependencies, including linked fixture-only benches.

<a id="rv-client-node-1cfb3796"></a>

### RV-CLIENT-NODE-1CFB3796 — No timeouts surfaced and no cancellation: JS callers get the core SDK's known unbounded-hang class as the *only* behavior

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-client-node`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-client-node/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.1](shamir-client-node/SUMMARY.md#review-3-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-client-node P0.4 (P0)](shamir-client-node/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Both timeout options are None, and there is no cancellation surface. A configured peer can stall TLS/SCRAM or withhold a response; Promise.race does not cancel the native operation. The reader can also mark closed/drain between roundtrip's initial check and later registration, leaving a new waiter if its write succeeds. Existing knobs only bound TCP establishment and post-write response waiting, not the complete operation.

### RV-CLIENT-93C26EBF — Reader exit never closes subscription channels: every live subscriber hangs forever on any disconnect

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-client`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-client/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-client/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-client/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-client/correctness-tdd.md#review-1); [shamir-client/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 2](shamir-client/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-2).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** After EOF the registry retains senders; each handle retains that registry. After queued items drain, next remains pending even after Client Drop. A regression must assert receive closure, not merely pending-map drain.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Natural reader exit clears neither senders nor early buffers; handles retain registry ownership.

<a id="rv-client-b42b8576"></a>

### RV-CLIENT-B42B8576 — Reader-exit drain races pending registration → permanent hang of an in-flight request

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-client`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-client/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-client/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-client/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 1](shamir-client/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-1); [shamir-client/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-client/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P1. Исходные пункты: [shamir-client P0.2 (P0)](shamir-client/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2); [shamir-client P1.6 (P1)](shamir-client/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-6).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** A caller passes the initial check, reader sets closed and drains, then caller inserts. If its write succeeds, no reader remains to resolve its retained sender. A protocol-close frame or half-closed transport can leave writing available. None timeout makes the wait indefinite.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** After the reader's final drain, a successful write leaves an unanswered sender under default None response timeout.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs:408](../../../../crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs); [crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs:1240](../../../../crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs); [crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs:1271](../../../../crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs); [crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs:1290](../../../../crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs).

<a id="rv-client-de75addd"></a>

### RV-CLIENT-DE75ADDD — `Client::resume` hands the bearer ticket to an unverified peer; `pinned_hash` is dead bookkeeping on the resume path

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-client`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-client/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.1](shamir-client/SUMMARY.md#review-3-1); [shamir-client/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 3](shamir-client/api-wire-protocol.md#review-3); [shamir-client/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 3](shamir-client/correctness-tdd.md#review-3); [shamir-client/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-client/security-crypto.md#review-1); [shamir-client/security-crypto.md · Claim Nits/client.rs:603](shamir-client/security-crypto.md#review-nits-client-rs-603).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P1. Исходные пункты: [shamir-client P0.3 (P0)](shamir-client/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3); [shamir-client P1.8 (P1)](shamir-client/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-8).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** An active endpoint substitute can receive a valid bearer ticket through accepted TLS and use it first against the real endpoint. Pin bookkeeping and fresh-exporter strength checks do not prevent disclosure. Checking resume_ok afterward is too late.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Ticket transmission follows unauthenticated TLS; only session-ID length is checked before the caller's pin is stored. Wrong-identity rejection after disclosure alone is not a sufficient oracle. It is not a client possession proof and is not independently erroneous.

<a id="rv-client-d4d604ca"></a>

### RV-CLIENT-D4D604CA — `unsafe`: none in this crate; dependency framing has a sound SAFETY argument

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** memory/ownership safety — scoped reachability. **Модули:** `shamir-client`, `shamir-transport-tcp`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-client/security-crypto.md · Claim NF.5](shamir-client/security-crypto.md#review-nf-5); [shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.1](shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md#review-3-1); [shamir-transport-tcp/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 8](shamir-transport-tcp/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-8); [shamir-transport-tcp/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-transport-tcp/security-crypto.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-transport-tcp P0.1 (P0)](shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Safe AsyncRead получает только initialized &mut [u8]; cancellation не открывает Vec с неинициализированными live bytes.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Client unsafe absence is true, but inherited framing is unsound: set_len exposes fresh uninitialized capacity before Tokio 1.49.0 read_exact constructs fully initialized ReadBuf::new. A safe AsyncRead may inspect initialized bytes; dropping a pending payload read also skips clear and returns access to an uninitialized Vec. Exact dependency source and pinned Rust 1.94 set_len contract provide positive counter-evidence, without a reproduction. Source: https://docs.rs/crate/tokio/1.49.0/source/src/io/util/read_exact.rs ; https://docs.rs/crate/tokio/1.49.0/source/src/io/read_buf.rs ; [Rust 1.94 contract](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rust-lang/rust/1.94.0/library/alloc/src/vec/mod.rs)

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** A legal reader can inspect those bytes; later clear cannot retroactively satisfy that precondition. Server discarded buffers do not prove a remote disclosure, but they do not make the generic API sound.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Clear на Err/после read не исправляет уже нарушенный Vec::set_len/ReadBuf::new precondition. Rust 1.95 source повторно подтверждает initialize-before-set_len закон.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Initialized resize/reuse либо корректный ReadBuf::uninit/spare-capacity протокол; set_len только после initialization и с полным cancel/drop ownership.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Legal AsyncRead inspecting initialized bytes и cancellation после partial payload; initialized state/Vec length post-drop должны быть sound. Miri/instrumented verification потребуется отдельно, здесь не запускалась.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs:118](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs:129](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs:314](../../../../crates/shamir-client/src/client.rs); [Cargo.lock:4195](../../../../Cargo.lock); [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs:124](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs:127](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/framer.rs:224](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/framer.rs).

### RV-COLLECTIONS-D5FF5F41 — No documented serialization/wire contract for TMap-backed protocol fields

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-collections`.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** No leaf serde/duplicate contract exists. A decoded map containing alias a twice retains one position and the later operation; the planner cannot recover multiplicity. <a id="rv-collections-91305482"></a>

### RV-COLLECTIONS-91305482 — TMap/TSet docs omit the O(N) order-preserving-removal asymmetry; consumers hit it on hot paths via the alias invisibly

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** structural cost — без измерений. **Модули:** `shamir-collections`, `shamir-tx`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-collections/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.1](shamir-collections/SUMMARY.md#review-4-1); [shamir-collections/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 1](shamir-collections/performance-hotpath.md#review-1); [shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.5](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#review-4-5); [shamir-tx/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 5](shamir-tx/performance-hotpath.md#review-5).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P2. Исходные пункты: [shamir-collections P0.2 (P0)](shamir-collections/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2); [shamir-tx P2.21 (P2)](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#plan-p2-21).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Pinned IndexMap 2.14.0 decrements later indices and removes from its dense Vec. MVCC snapshot_le returns key-sorted winners, collected into TMap; matching key-major history groups can repeatedly remove the first remaining winner, yielding N(N−1)/2 entry shifts. This proves structural worst-case work, not measured latency. Replacing shift_remove blindly changes the remaining overlay drain order. Source: https://docs.rs/crate/indexmap/2.14.0/source/src/inner.rs.

<a id="rv-connect-0a42f42c"></a>

### RV-CONNECT-0A42F42C — Capped login evicts active sessions instead of implementing the specified all-active rejection

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-connect`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.4](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-4).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-connect P0.4 (P0)](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** AUTH §7.4 requires rejection of a new login when all 16 sessions were active within one minute. insert_with_per_user_cap has no current-time/active classification and always removes the smallest last_activity value once count reaches the cap. Production full SCRAM calls this helper. A user with valid credentials can displace an actively used session; this is not unauthenticated account takeover. Existing tests assert unconditional LRU and omit the all-active branch. A regression oracle needs 16 recently active sessions and must expect rejection with no eviction.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [docs/guide-docs/client-server-protocol-spec/AUTH_PROTOCOL.md:454](../../../guide-docs/client-server-protocol-spec/AUTH_PROTOCOL.md); [crates/shamir-connect/src/server/session.rs:454](../../../../crates/shamir-connect/src/server/session.rs); [crates/shamir-connect/src/server/session.rs:485](../../../../crates/shamir-connect/src/server/session.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:580](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-connect/tests/integration_session.rs:381](../../../../crates/shamir-connect/tests/integration_session.rs).

<a id="rv-connect-fed6910e"></a>

### RV-CONNECT-FED6910E — client feature cannot build without server

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-connect`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.4](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-1-4); [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.1](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-5-1); [shamir-connect/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 1](shamir-connect/api-wire-protocol.md#review-1); [shamir-connect/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 4](shamir-connect/correctness-tdd.md#review-4).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-connect P0.7 (P0)](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-7).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** README's standalone client recipe removes server registration while every client flow imports server types. Default/workspace feature unification masks this configuration defect; no compilation was performed.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Feature-gated server registration conflicts with unconditional client imports; default feature unification is not a standalone-client oracle.

<a id="rv-connect-d8c2825a"></a>

### RV-CONNECT-D8C2825A — SessionStore::cap_lock: unjustified parking_lot::Mutex held across an O(all-sessions) scan

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-connect`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.2](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-2-2); [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.1](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-4-1); [shamir-connect/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 2](shamir-connect/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-2); [shamir-connect/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 1](shamir-connect/performance-hotpath.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-connect P0.4 (P0)](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Every successful capped login serializes a full map traversal and matching-user sort. Production reaches it after authentication; connection/subnet limits constrain load. Scaling and cross-user serialization are proven, but High availability impact and numeric latency are not.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Successful capped production login serializes O(N) traversal across unrelated users; High impact remains unmeasured and deployment-bounded. The scan covers all users and holds the global creation lock; exact deployed availability impact remains unmeasured.

### RV-CONNECT-0FC58D93 — TOFU pin callback fires before Ed25519 identity verification completes

### RV-CONNECT-ECE3E157 — FjallConsumedCounters::try_advance conflates persistence failure with replay and logs nothing

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-connect`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.1](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-6-1); [shamir-connect/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-connect/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Connect's bool result loses get/insert/persist causes and adds no diagnostics. Exact fjall 3.1.6 publishes the successful insert before persist; persist failure logs and poisons the database, preventing later family writes. Thus total operator silence is false. Source: https://docs.rs/crate/fjall/3.1.6/source/src/db.rs and src/keyspace/mod.rs.

### RV-CONNECT-8AD46452 — Public dispatch_request silently skips the post-auth rate limit

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-connect`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.5](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-1-5); [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.1](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-3-1); [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.2](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-5-2); [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.4](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-6-4); [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 7.1](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-7-1); [shamir-connect/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 2](shamir-connect/api-wire-protocol.md#review-2); [shamir-connect/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 5](shamir-connect/correctness-tdd.md#review-5); [shamir-connect/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 4](shamir-connect/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-4); [shamir-connect/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-connect/security-crypto.md#review-1); [shamir-connect/style-claude-md.md · Claim 1](shamir-connect/style-claude-md.md#review-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** A valid stored sid sent through owned dispatch reaches the handler regardless of a depleted bucket. The shipped server uses view dispatch, so present production requests do not take this bypass.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Only the borrowed-view path enforces post-auth request tokens. The documented equivalence is false because only view dispatch enforces the bucket. Only view dispatch gates requests; production selects view.

<a id="rv-db-64ef85ba"></a>

### RV-DB-64EF85BA — SystemStore catalogue WAL is not replayed before boot reads

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-db`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-db P0.3 (P0)](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** SystemStore::init only calls DbInstance::add_repo, whose factory path constructs registrations without replay. ShamirDb immediately reads catalogue tables. A process crash after a catalogue commit reaches WAL but before history drain can therefore leave surviving committed metadata invisible at boot. Version-floor scanning reads WAL numbers, not values. Lazy drainer seeding may recover later after another operation starts it, too late for the already-built serving set. No crash/reopen experiment was run.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/system_store.rs:112](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/system_store.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/core.rs:194](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/core.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/db_instance/db_instance.rs:52](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/db_instance/db_instance.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/repo/repo_instance.rs:719](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/repo/repo_instance.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/tx/drainer.rs:1013](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/tx/drainer.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/tx/recovery.rs:329](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/tx/recovery.rs).

<a id="rv-db-b40771a1"></a>

### RV-DB-B40771A1 — Host source compilation blocks the async caller directly

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-db`, `shamir-wasm-host`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.5](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-5); [shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.7](shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md#review-1-7); [shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-wasm-host/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 2](shamir-wasm-host/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-2); [shamir-wasm-host/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 4](shamir-wasm-host/correctness-tdd.md#review-4).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P2. Исходные пункты: [shamir-db P2.16 (P2)](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#plan-p2-16); [shamir-wasm-host P0.4 (P0)](shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Both async creation methods directly call synchronous compile_rust_source. Its implementation performs filesystem work, toolchain probes and child.wait_timeout on the caller thread, without a facade spawn_blocking boundary. A permitted source submission therefore blocks a Tokio worker; a current-thread embedder blocks its whole runtime. This contradicts the inspected async/offload rule, without claiming measured starvation duration or CPU usage.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Authorized source submission or permitted binary DDL can block a worker; exact starvation duration is unmeasured.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [CLAUDE.md:338](../../../../CLAUDE.md); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/function_management.rs:172](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/function_management.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs:221](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs); [crates/shamir-wasm-host/src/compile.rs:454](../../../../crates/shamir-wasm-host/src/compile.rs); [crates/shamir-wasm-host/src/compile.rs:592](../../../../crates/shamir-wasm-host/src/compile.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs:232](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/function_management.rs:183](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/function_management.rs).

<a id="rv-db-5aaddfef"></a>

### RV-DB-5AADDFEF — `replace=true` on a WASM validator destroys persisted binding bookkeeping and can silently re-key its identity

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-db`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.9](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-1-9); [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.1](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-5-1); [shamir-db/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 1](shamir-db/api-wire-protocol.md#review-1); [shamir-db/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 9](shamir-db/correctness-tdd.md#review-9).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-db P0.4 (P0)](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Replace saves bound_in=[] and removes the live entry, erasing its reverse binding set. Drop then succeeds despite persisted table-side bindings; those writes fail closed after removal. The discriminating regression must replace an already-bound validator, preserve its ID/bookkeeping, and still refuse drop.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** WASM replacement derives identity only from live registration, persists empty bound_in and removes/re-registers. Table-side bindings remain; subsequent missing-validator writes fail closed rather than silently bypass validation.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs:249](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/validator/registry.rs:156](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/validator/registry.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs:284](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs:305](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/validator_management.rs); [crates/shamir-db/tests/validators_e2e.rs:488](../../../../crates/shamir-db/tests/validators_e2e.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/table/table_manager_validators.rs:331](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/table/table_manager_validators.rs).

### RV-DB-078FD12C — `DROP DATABASE ... CASCADE` executed against a different database destroys the *batch's* database's tables

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-db`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-db/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-db/correctness-tdd.md#review-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** With matching repository/table names in A and B, execution on A enumerates B but deletes A and cleans A's validator references. Ordinary top-level wire DDL is superuser-gated; facade calls and a permitted ForEach body retain the mismatch. Oracle: A's rows/bindings survive while only B disappears.

### RV-DB-E301E1EF — `remove_group_member` on a nonexistent group *creates* a phantom group record (and the wire remove path lacks the existence guard the add path has)

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-db`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.2](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-1-2); [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.5](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-2-5); [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.4](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-6-4); [shamir-db/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 5](shamir-db/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-5); [shamir-db/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 2](shamir-db/correctness-tdd.md#review-2); [shamir-db/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 4](shamir-db/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** An accepted numeric missing group ID reaches defaults and unconditional save_group. Root-management authority is required; this is not unauthenticated escalation. The existing regression removes a nonexistent user from an existing group, not a missing group.

### RV-DB-6540DC7B — Catalogue-persistence failures are swallowed (`warn!` + continue) across the DB/repo/table lifecycle, so multi-step mutations can return `Ok(())` half-migrated

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-db`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.11](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-5-11); [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.1](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-6-1); [shamir-db/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 11](shamir-db/api-wire-protocol.md#review-11); [shamir-db/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-db/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-db P0.3 (P0)](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Registry mutation precedes catalogue writes, and failed save-new does not prevent remove-old. Several prerequisite loads propagate errors after live mutation. The add_table documentation admits best effort, but that does not make rename preservation or successful wire durability true.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Warn-only save failure still returns DbInstance and created=true. create_db_as returns DbInstance after warn-only save failure; the handler reports created=true.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/db_management.rs:58](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/db_management.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/execute/admin_db_repo.rs:70](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/execute/admin_db_repo.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/db_management.rs:184](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/db_management.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/table_management.rs:337](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/table_management.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/execute/admin_db_repo.rs:67](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/execute/admin_db_repo.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/db_management.rs:195](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/db_management.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/table_management.rs:323](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/table_management.rs).

### RV-DB-093EAA95 — `rename_function_as` / `rename_validator_as` / `rename_function_folder_as` destroy the durable record *before* writing the new one (remove-before-write)

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-db`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.6](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-1-6); [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.2](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-6-2); [shamir-db/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 6](shamir-db/correctness-tdd.md#review-6); [shamir-db/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 2](shamir-db/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-db P0.3 (P0)](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Function/validator deletion precedes save; folder rename deletes the entire old subtree before its save loop. Its no-partial-state promise is false. Fault timing must distinguish failed-before-commit from committed-but-deferred outcomes.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Preserving an old row is necessary but insufficient for atomic rename or live/catalogue consistency.

### RV-DB-C9444758 — Guest-controlled header values/method can inject arbitrary curl config directives (CRLF injection)

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-db`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.1](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-3-1); [shamir-db/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-db/security-crypto.md#review-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** For granted egress, a header value containing LF, an unquoted directive, LF and # escapes onto separate config lines. [curl 8.10.1 parsing](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/curl/curl/curl-8_10_1/src/tool_parsecfg.c) accepts the unterminated quoted first line; the suffix comments out the appended quote. Deployed-version effects remain unmeasured.

<a id="rv-engine-4cf4df32"></a>

### RV-ENGINE-4CF4DF32 — Two remediation regression files are not registered; the pre-read test also claims a nonexistent get injector

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped coverage/oracle. **Модули:** `shamir-engine`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-engine/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-engine/SUMMARY.md#new-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P1. Исходные пункты: [shamir-engine P0.2 (P0)](shamir-engine/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2); [shamir-engine P1.7 (P1)](shamir-engine/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-7); [shamir-engine P1.10 (P1)](shamir-engine/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-10).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Neither file appears in the table manifest. get never checks the armed hook; read_one_tx does, but update_tx swallows that injected error. Registration alone is insufficient for the delete/set fixtures.

### RV-FUNCLIB-64320F3C — Global `std::sync::Mutex` regex cache on the hot filter path — regex compiled while holding the lock

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-funclib`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.6](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-4-6); [shamir-funclib/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 1](shamir-funclib/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-1); [shamir-funclib/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 6](shamir-funclib/performance-hotpath.md#review-6).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-funclib P0.4 (P0)](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Every hit locks one global mutex; misses compile while holding it. Inserting a 257th pattern clears the cache, causing subsequent churn. Serialization is proved, but throughput collapse and compile durations are unmeasured.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Published scc 3.8.4 read_sync is shared-locked, not the historical recipe's lock-free lookup.

### RV-FUNCLIB-5FA455F7 — Two coexisting equality semantics: `==` (variant-strict) vs `compare` (value-strict)

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-funclib`, `shamir-types`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.5](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-1-5); [shamir-funclib/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 5](shamir-funclib/correctness-tdd.md#review-5); [shamir-types/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-types/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-types/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.4](shamir-types/SUMMARY.md#review-5-4); [shamir-types/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 4](shamir-types/api-wire-protocol.md#review-4); [shamir-types/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-types/correctness-tdd.md#review-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Cross-type array equality is intentionally variant-sensitive and aggregate equality is compare-based. The separate concrete defect is +0.0==-0.0 with different hash bit inputs, breaking the hash-based distinct contract; no signed-zero oracle exists.

### RV-FUNCLIB-CDACB2E5 — Unbounded-allocation scalar paths: `strings/repeat`, `strings/pad_left`/`pad_right`, `gen/random_bytes`

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-funclib`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.2](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-3-2); [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.5](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-4-5); [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.1](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-5-1); [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.2](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-6-2); [shamir-funclib/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 1](shamir-funclib/api-wire-protocol.md#review-1); [shamir-funclib/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-funclib/correctness-tdd.md#review-1); [shamir-funclib/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 2](shamir-funclib/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-2); [shamir-funclib/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 5](shamir-funclib/performance-hotpath.md#review-5); [shamir-funclib/security-crypto.md · Claim 2](shamir-funclib/security-crypto.md#review-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-funclib P0.1 (P0)](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Only negativity is rejected before result allocation. Checked multiplication, checked platform-size conversion and byte ceilings are absent; pad additionally builds intermediate fill/output strings. Existing tests do not assert upper-bound rejection.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** A positive i64 size reaches vec directly; only negative values are rejected. No checked output-size or positive ceiling exists before allocations; existing tests cover only small outputs and negatives. Neither input-frame size nor later response limits bound this output amplification.

<a id="rv-funclib-84dd4da9"></a>

### RV-FUNCLIB-84DD4DA9 — `validate/is_json` hand-rolled parser recurses without a depth cap — stack-overflow abort from a ~20 KB query string

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-funclib`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.1](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-3-1); [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.1](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-6-1); [shamir-funclib/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-funclib/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1); [shamir-funclib/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-funclib/security-crypto.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-funclib P0.1 (P0)](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Query-reachable nested JSON-in-Str validation имеет заранее определённый semantic recursion/resource bound до stack walk.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Arbitrary nesting inside an evaluated string drives native mutual recursion without a counter. Transport nesting checks do not inspect that text. Availability risk is established; the title's 20-KB crash threshold and unauthenticated exposure are not.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Custom value↔array/object recursion остаётся uncapped; precise abort depth/stack margin не измерялся.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Использовать доказанно bounded parser/iterative walk с явным accepted-depth/error policy; outer rmp-serde container limit не видит JSON внутри Str. Не заменять exhaustion ложным claims об invalid JSON без согласованного контракта.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Авторизованный обычный scalar-expression path, глубоко вложенный valid JSON в Str: ограниченный coded outcome ДО destructive recursion; shallow valid/invalid JSON controls. Safe bounded fixture, не попытка реально вызвать stack overflow.

<a id="rv-index-2f3cbca3"></a>

### RV-INDEX-2F3CBCA3 — Restart reseeds next_delta_idx below the persisted absorption watermark after pruning

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-index`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-index/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.2](shamir-index/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-index P0.4 (P0)](shamir-index/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Restart delta allocator не переиспользует indices ниже persisted absorption boundary.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Take a valid flipped manifest with delta_applied_upto=3 and all chunks below 3 pruned. Restore scans no remaining chunks, gets highest_delta_index=0, and sets next_delta_idx=1, ignoring the manifest boundary. The next committed mutation writes chunk 1. A second restart loads the old base and replay skips 1 because 1<3, silently losing the new mutation from search. This requires neither corruption nor a racing snapshot. The registered generation-flip test stops after the first restart; a discriminating oracle must append after that restore and reopen again. Reseeding must be at least the persisted absorption boundary and must not conceal scan errors.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Первый restore корректен, но empty remaining scan seed=1 игнорирует manifest; новое chunk1 затем skip как already absorbed.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Seed next_delta_idx как минимум manifest absorption/recovered-tail boundary и не маскировать scan errors нулём; сохранять ordered replay.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Flip с delta_applied_upto=3, prune старых chunks, reopen, append новую mutation, второй reopen — membership сохраняется; oracle заканчивающийся первым reopen не различает дефект.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/vector_backend.rs:703](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/vector_backend.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/vector_backend.rs:774](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/vector_backend.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/snapshot.rs:1240](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/snapshot.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/snapshot.rs:1348](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/snapshot.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/tests/delta_log_tests.rs:354](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/tests/delta_log_tests.rs).

- **Прочитанные original source line fragments (indent stripped; separator /):** [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/vector_backend.rs:705](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/vector_backend.rs): <code>let hwm = snapshot::highest_delta_index(&amp;info_store, &amp;keyspace) / .await / .unwrap_or(0);</code>; [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/vector_backend.rs:774](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/vector_backend.rs): <code>let idx = self.next_delta_idx.fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel); / snapshot::append_delta(info_store, &amp;keyspace, idx, &amp;ops) / .await</code>.

- **Зависимости / связанные карточки:** [RV-INDEX-216A1B0E](P1.md#rv-index-216a1b0e) — Applied watermark до flip и allocator reseed после restart — разные transitions.

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-numa`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.3](shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md#review-4-3); [shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.1](shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md#review-5-1); [shamir-numa/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 1](shamir-numa/api-wire-protocol.md#review-1); [shamir-numa/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 1](shamir-numa/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-1); [shamir-numa/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-numa/correctness-tdd.md#review-1); [shamir-numa/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 3](shamir-numa/performance-hotpath.md#review-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** With two replicas, A publishes and captures X1; B publishes and mirrors X2; A writes X1 last to replica 1. - **Counter-evidence / границы:** Physical remoteness and numerical latency remain unmeasured.

<a id="rv-numa-c54e6a13"></a>

### RV-NUMA-C54E6A13 — Concurrency test asserts node 0 only -- the mirror half of `rcu` has zero multi-threaded coverage

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped coverage/oracle. **Модули:** `shamir-numa`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.2](shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md#review-1-2); [shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.2](shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md#review-2-2); [shamir-numa/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 2](shamir-numa/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-2); [shamir-numa/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 2](shamir-numa/correctness-tdd.md#review-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-numa P0.2 (P0)](shamir-numa/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** The eight-thread test joins writers and asserts only node 0. Sequential all-replica assertions catch missing mirroring, not reordered mirrors. A deterministic delayed-mirror oracle must inspect every replica and mixed publication methods.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Only node 0 is asserted after joins; the stale-mirror schedule does not falsify that assertion. The registered concurrency test only checks node 0, with no mixed-writer final mirror assertion. The registered concurrent test inspects only canonical node 0. Its increment oracle is meaningful but remains unchanged if every concurrent mirror is stale; sequential mirror tests do not force that counterexample.

Main reaches invocation context, but function Execute authorization and effective-actor gateway access remain.

### RV-QUERY-BUILDER-MACROS-A63844FD — Zero negative/error-path tests; the entire macro diagnostic surface is unverified (TDD "Red" missing for every error path)

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped coverage/oracle. **Модули:** `shamir-query-builder-macros`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-query-builder-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.3](shamir-query-builder-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-3-3); [shamir-query-builder-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.1](shamir-query-builder-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-6-1); [shamir-query-builder-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 7.3](shamir-query-builder-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-7-3); [shamir-query-builder-macros/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 3](shamir-query-builder-macros/correctness-tdd.md#review-3); [shamir-query-builder-macros/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-query-builder-macros/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1); [shamir-query-builder-macros/security-crypto.md · Claim 3](shamir-query-builder-macros/security-crypto.md#review-3); [shamir-query-builder-macros/style-claude-md.md · Claim 3](shamir-query-builder-macros/style-claude-md.md#review-3).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P2. Исходные пункты: [shamir-query-builder-macros P0.1 (P0)](shamir-query-builder-macros/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1); [shamir-query-builder-macros P2.8 (P2)](shamir-query-builder-macros/SUMMARY.md#plan-p2-8).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** No registered rejection harness exists. Deleting unknown-name rejection or accepting malformed groups would not be caught by accepted-input tests. Default count alias, literal write-table and bare-variable RHS cases also lack pins. Historical Red activity cannot be inferred from absence.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Only the rejection/arity facet is unpinned. Existing accepted-input tests cannot detect rejection-only mutations; neither local absence nor historical green labels establishes past TDD behavior. This is DSL-confinement coverage, not a security boundary against an author who can supply arbitrary Rust RHS code.

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-query-builder`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P2. Исходные пункты: [shamir-query-builder P0.1 (P0)](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1); [shamir-query-builder P2.14 (P2)](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#plan-p2-14).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Register known alias a and a read b whose only a reference is a SELECT function argument or HAVING operand. Builder try_build accepts it, but BatchPlanner's Read arm extracts only WHERE references. build_resolved_refs exposes results only for DataFlow/Both edges, so HAVING receives no a result unless another genuine reference independently supplies that edge. SELECT has an additional deterministic failure: SelectProjection evaluates with its own permanently empty reference map, so even an added dependency cannot supply a. An identity-like SELECT expression over a.first().field('x') therefore resolves to Null; a HAVING comparison against that reference can wrongly remove groups. Explicit after is not a data grant and is insufficient. Existing builder tests only reject nonexistent aliases; a discriminating integration oracle must use a known non-null producer result through real batch planning/execution and assert the projected value/group retention.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-query-builder/src/batch/batch.rs:1271](../../../../crates/shamir-query-builder/src/batch/batch.rs); [crates/shamir-query-builder/src/batch/batch.rs:1290](../../../../crates/shamir-query-builder/src/batch/batch.rs); [crates/shamir-query-builder/src/batch/tests/batch_tests.rs:363](../../../../crates/shamir-query-builder/src/batch/tests/batch_tests.rs); [crates/shamir-query-builder/src/batch/tests/batch_tests.rs:392](../../../../crates/shamir-query-builder/src/batch/tests/batch_tests.rs); [crates/shamir-query-types/src/batch/planner.rs:277](../../../../crates/shamir-query-types/src/batch/planner.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/query/batch/query_runner.rs:54](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/query/batch/query_runner.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/query/read/select_projection.rs:207](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/query/read/select_projection.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/query/read/select_projection.rs:242](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/query/read/select_projection.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/query/read/select_projection.rs:273](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/query/read/select_projection.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/query/read/aggregate.rs:1304](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/query/read/aggregate.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/query/filter/resolve.rs:363](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/query/filter/resolve.rs).

### RV-QUERY-BUILDER-2FD16C76 — `Batch::after` and `Batch::when` silently no-op when the handle's alias is not registered in this batch

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** memory/ownership safety — scoped reachability. **Модули:** `shamir-query-builder`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.2](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#review-1-2); [shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.2](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#review-5-2); [shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.2](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#review-6-2); [shamir-query-builder/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 2](shamir-query-builder/api-wire-protocol.md#review-2); [shamir-query-builder/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 2](shamir-query-builder/correctness-tdd.md#review-2); [shamir-query-builder/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 2](shamir-query-builder/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-query-builder P0.2 (P0)](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Passing a foreign missing dependent alias records nothing and returns self; try_build cannot diagnose discarded intent. A foreign equal alias mutates the local entry. Tests inject bad references into existing entries, which does not exercise lookup misses. Replacement is a separate mechanism.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Alias-only handles cannot distinguish equal-name foreign operations; lookup misses lose mutation intent. Lookup misses record neither mutation nor error.

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-query-builder`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.3](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#review-1-3); [shamir-query-builder/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 3](shamir-query-builder/correctness-tdd.md#review-3).

### RV-QUERY-BUILDER-20D9732A — `Doc::set` `.expect()`s the `FilterValue` -&gt; `QueryValue` msgpack round-trip in a public setter

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-query-builder`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.5](shamir-query-builder/SUMMARY.md#review-6-5); [shamir-query-builder/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 5](shamir-query-builder/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-5).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** FilterValue::Array can be locally nested beyond the exact codec's active QueryValue decoder budget. Encoding succeeds absent resource exhaustion, decode returns DepthLimitExceeded, and the setter expects success. Literal fast-path conversion would remove this particular codec failure but must address its own recursion and expression fallback.

<a id="rv-query-types-8257acb4"></a>

### RV-QUERY-TYPES-8257ACB4 — Unbounded recursive walks over already-parsed attacker trees

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-query-types`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.2](shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md#review-3-2); [shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.1](shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md#review-5-1); [shamir-query-types/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-query-types/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1); [shamir-query-types/security-crypto.md · Claim 2](shamir-query-types/security-crypto.md#review-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P2. Исходные пункты: [shamir-query-types 2 (P0)](shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md#plan-2); [shamir-query-types 19 (P2)](shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md#plan-19).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** An acyclic long alias chain is visited recursively before TooDeep is evaluated. Value/filter recursion also precedes engine validation. Default outer max_queries=100 limits that graph witness, while nested limits and configurable caps differ; no exact remotely reachable stack-abort threshold was proved.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Planner extraction runs before engine filter validation; default outer query caps and codec depth constrain remote witnesses. High operator caps or uncapped nested bodies are prerequisites; exact abort thresholds remain unmeasured.

### RV-QUERY-TYPES-40186F82 — Destructive-op HMAC gate never reaches ops nested in `Batch`/`ForEach`; `is_admin()` misclassifies `ForEach` via non-exhaustive `matches!`

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-query-types`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.4](shamir-query-types/SUMMARY.md#review-3-4); [shamir-query-types/security-crypto.md · Claim 4](shamir-query-types/security-crypto.md#review-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** check_destructive_hmacs iterates only outer queries; Batch/ForEach fall through. A superuser's nested DropDb can therefore reach an admin handler without its tag. Non-superuser paths retain actor DAC, so the separate ForEach coarse-policy omission is not unrestricted escalation.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Only outer entries are checked; container bodies continue through nested execution without another confirmation check. check_destructive_hmacs iterates only outer queries; Batch/ForEach fall through.

### RV-SDK-MACROS-D3AD2579 — Generated standard-library names remain caller-shadowable

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `low`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-sdk-macros`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.2](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-sdk-macros P0.1 (P0)](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** All allocators emit bare Vec<u8>; validator also emits bare Option/Some/None, and core paths lack a leading root qualifier. A consumer with an otherwise valid qualified procedure signature and a module-local nongeneric Vec changes resolution of the generated allocator annotation and prevents compilation. This is independent of Cargo dependency renaming. quote 1.0.45 generates call-site spans: https://docs.rs/crate/quote/1.0.45/source/src/lib.rs; the Rust Reference confirms unhygienic inline resolution: https://doc.rust-lang.org/reference/procedural-macros.html. A consumer compile-pass fixture shadowing these names would catch the defect.

### RV-SDK-MACROS-FEC0E5E4 — Guest executor has no suspension protocol for a genuinely Pending future

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-sdk`, `shamir-sdk-macros`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.8](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-1-8); [shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.3](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-4-3); [shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.7](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-6-7); [shamir-sdk-macros/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 1](shamir-sdk-macros/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-1); [shamir-sdk-macros/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 8](shamir-sdk-macros/correctness-tdd.md#review-8); [shamir-sdk-macros/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 7](shamir-sdk-macros/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-7); [shamir-sdk-macros/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 3](shamir-sdk-macros/performance-hotpath.md#review-3); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.4](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-1-4); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.3](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-4-3); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.8](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-6-8); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 7.2](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-7-2); [shamir-sdk/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 1](shamir-sdk/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-1); [shamir-sdk/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 4](shamir-sdk/correctness-tdd.md#review-4); [shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 9](shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-9); [shamir-sdk/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 3](shamir-sdk/performance-hotpath.md#review-3); [shamir-sdk/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-sdk/security-crypto.md#review-1); [shamir-sdk/style-claude-md.md · Claim 2](shamir-sdk/style-claude-md.md#review-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P1. Исходные пункты: [shamir-sdk-macros P1.7 (P1)](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-7); [shamir-sdk P0.2 (P0)](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** A never-ready guest future repeatedly reaches spin_loop without parking or a local poll bound. wasmtime 46.0.2 host futures instead suspend through src/runtime/fiber.rs:291: https://docs.rs/crate/wasmtime/46.0.2/source/src/runtime/fiber.rs. Fuel and an independently driven epoch deadline bound ordinary deployment execution, not measured CPU percentages.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Unbounded guest-local polling remains; async host callbacks already suspend fibers rather than necessarily pending the guest future. Persistent guest-local Pending busy-polls until interruption; an exact CPU percentage and every-Pending exhaustion claim are unsupported. The helper has no local bound, but fuel/epoch limits and async host fiber suspension invalidate the unconditional production-hang scenario. Native use is unbounded; default WASM fuel bounds the loop. Unresolved futures busy-poll; later Ready terminates and default fuel is finite. Always-Pending consumes finite default WASM fuel; Pending-then-Ready completes. Pure-only/future-host-import comments remain despite all four wrappers and present imports.

### RV-SDK-MACROS-4DB0D871 — `#[validator]` silently coerces undecodable params into `record = Null`, `old_record = None`

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-sdk-macros`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.1](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-3-1); [shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.2](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-5-2); [shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.4](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#review-6-4); [shamir-sdk-macros/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 2](shamir-sdk-macros/api-wire-protocol.md#review-2); [shamir-sdk-macros/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 4](shamir-sdk-macros/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-4); [shamir-sdk-macros/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-sdk-macros/security-crypto.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-sdk-macros P0.3 (P0)](shamir-sdk-macros/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Reserved/malformed MessagePack or a valid non-map produces empty Params and then Null/None. The engine supplies record and old_record normally, including legitimate deletion Null. Whether an abnormal payload permits a write depends on the validator body. An oracle must reject malformed input while accepting a valid empty parameter map where allowed.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Params::get only reports missing keys; the original decode cause was already discarded by decode_params.

<a id="rv-sdk-8190492d"></a>

### RV-SDK-8190492D — Host-import ABI leaks both directions' buffers on every call — unbounded guest linear-memory growth in loops

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-sdk`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.7](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-3-7); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.1](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-4-1); [shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 3](shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-3); [shamir-sdk/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 1](shamir-sdk/performance-hotpath.md#review-1); [shamir-sdk/security-crypto.md · Claim 8](shamir-sdk/security-crypto.md#review-8).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-sdk P0.3 (P0)](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Repeated payload calls forget request Vecs and allocated response bytes even when decoded values are dropped. A finite Store cap eventually prevents more allocations. This is invocation-local availability/correctness debt, not unlimited host-process leakage; exact failure thresholds are unmeasured.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Internal comments mention short-lived allocations, but public host-call APIs do not explain cumulative loop retention or reliance on the host cap. Store limits/drop contain the scope; no separate error cleanup exists.

### RV-SDK-A68C70DE — Fail-open protocol handling: decode/encode failures silently become plausible success values (7 decode sites + `decode_params` + `encode_value` + `Table::insert`'s message)

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-sdk`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.2](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-1-2); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.3](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-3-3); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.3](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-5-3); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.1](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-6-1); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.3](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-6-3); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.5](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-6-5); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.6](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-6-6); [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.7](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#review-6-7); [shamir-sdk/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 3](shamir-sdk/api-wire-protocol.md#review-3); [shamir-sdk/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 2](shamir-sdk/correctness-tdd.md#review-2); [shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1); [shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 4](shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-4); [shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 6](shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-6); [shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 7](shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-7); [shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 8](shamir-sdk/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-8); [shamir-sdk/security-crypto.md · Claim 4](shamir-sdk/security-crypto.md#review-4).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P1. Исходные пункты: [shamir-sdk P0.1 (P0)](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1); [shamir-sdk P1.6 (P1)](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-6).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Seven response fallbacks and empty-Params fallback remain. Query corruption becomes success; insert/HTTP lose cause while failing. Exact rmp-serde 1.3.1 also supplies a present allocation-error path into empty encoding. Oracles must separately assert zero sentinel, encoded Null, malformed bytes, and allocation/codec failure.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Nonzero malformed query bytes become a successful empty list; HTTP/insert reject their fallback Null but discard the codec cause. The shipped host encodes actual Null nonzero and traps, rather than returning zero, on callee failure. A body ignoring parameters can then execute; an accessor reports missing rather than decode failure. Insert/HTTP reject fallback Null but lose the source; Table::get has no Result channel. Callee traps are documented intentional behavior; catchable composition is optional.
