<a id="rv-server-17329fc9"></a>

### RV-SERVER-17329FC9 — Bootstrap-token TTL check uses authentication-start time after awaiting proof

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-server`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-server/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.3](shamir-server/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-3).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Прямого явного plan item для собственного root нет; это распределение текущего не-P решения, а не новая исходная маркировка.

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Bootstrap TTL проверяется по времени authentication grant после proof, не по старому pre-challenge timestamp.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** run_handshake captures now_ns before sending the challenge, waits for proof, then passes the old timestamp to read_bootstrap_token_state. For t_init < token_expiry <= t_proof, metadata is readable and still active, and no intervening rotation occurred, the expiry predicate is false despite expiry before the session grant. A holder of the bootstrap secret can start before expiration and finish afterward; the unbounded proof wait widens the window, but the timestamp defect also exists with a finite wait. This is not passwordless or arbitrary-principal access. The scheduler does not perform the boot-only token rotation sweep. Existing caller-copy unit tests and an already-expired-before-login e2e case do not discriminate this boundary. Refresh time at the post-proof security decision and test expiry between challenge and proof through the actual handshake.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Metadata I/O fail closed; старый now_ns после await ещё передаётся read_bootstrap_token_state.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Свежая time decision непосредственно перед grant с существующей rotation/persist policy; bounded proof wait не заменяет свежесть.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** t_init<expiry≤t_proof, valid secret/proof и healthy unchanged metadata → отказ до session grant; already expired-before-init не различает stale now_ns.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:210](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:291](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:431](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/server_meta.rs:509](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/server_meta.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/server_meta.rs:524](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/server_meta.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/tests/connection_tests.rs:240](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/tests/connection_tests.rs); [crates/shamir-server/tests/bootstrap_token_lifecycle_e2e.rs:372](../../../../crates/shamir-server/tests/bootstrap_token_lifecycle_e2e.rs).

<a id="rv-server-c73466a7"></a>

### RV-SERVER-C73466A7 — Interactive-tx path bypasses the read-only-replica gate entirely

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-server`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-server/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-server/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-server/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-server/correctness-tdd.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-server P0.1 (P0)](shamir-server/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Явно настроенный NodeMode::ReadOnly блокирует записи во всех execute и interactive transaction entry points.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** With an explicitly ReadOnly handler and a principal authorized to write the target resources, TxBegin → TxExecute(insert) → TxCommit reaches the engine without NodeMode rejection. Database/table authorization still applies. Registered node-mode tests call Execute, so they do not catch this path.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Execute gate есть; interactive handlers проходят через actor-aware engine без mode check. Это не отсутствие authorization на ресурсы вообще.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Применить одинаковый mode invariant на фактическом TxBegin/TxExecute/TxCommit write route при сохранении actor DAC и разрешённых reads; не считать только Execute gate достаточным.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** ReadOnly handler, principal с обычным Store/table write правом, BEGIN→EXECUTE INSERT→COMMIT: reject до landing; отдельно read-only tx и обычный Execute контроль. NodeMode Primary/default — иной scope.

### RV-SERVER-227C178A — Dead follower-loop registry entries block resubscription after a journal gap

The existing gap test never tests restart.

<a id="rv-storage-e70a0ddc"></a>

### RV-STORAGE-E70A0DDC — CachedStore: unordered, non-atomic cache-mutation sites can leave the cache permanently behind inner

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-storage`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-storage/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-storage/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-storage/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 1](shamir-storage/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-storage P0.2 (P0)](shamir-storage/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** После завершения same-key writers cache и backing сходятся к одному последнему committed значению.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Force backing A then B, publish cache B then A: cache returns A after both calls while inner returns B. Async queued writes also bypass ordering with direct transact. Distinct-key tests cannot detect either witness.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Current cache_upsert имеет remove/insert и независимые publication sites; existing distinct-key tests не доказывают ordering.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Упорядочить backing/cache mutation во всех Sync/Async/transact/fill/reload routes, а не только сделать remove+insert атомарным; size считать только по действительным mutations.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Backing A затем B, cache publication B затем A; после обоих acknowledgements сравнить cache и raw backing, повторить mixed async/transact path. Distinct keys не различают order.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_cached.rs:431](../../../../crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_cached.rs); [crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_cached.rs:671](../../../../crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_cached.rs); [crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_cached.rs:403](../../../../crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_cached.rs).

<a id="rv-storage-98f6650d"></a>

### RV-STORAGE-98F6650D — MemBufferStore::get_many cache-fill can poison a Tombstone over a concurrent write — the #539 bug class survives in the vectored read

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-storage`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-storage/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-storage/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-storage/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.2](shamir-storage/SUMMARY.md#review-2-2); [shamir-storage/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 2](shamir-storage/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-2); [shamir-storage/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-storage/correctness-tdd.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-storage P0.1 (P0)](shamir-storage/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Vectored miss fill не скрывает acknowledged buffered write stale None/Live записью в cache-first read path.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** With K absent in inner, pause get_many after deciding None, acknowledge set(K,V), then complete the stale fill. Later wrapper get returns NotFound even after flush. No stale dirty_count assumption is required: get_many probes dirty directly before the writer.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Cache-first reads persistently hide the dirty value; shared tests cover only ordinary behavior.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Sibling get rechecks dirty; get_many fill по-прежнему публикует inner result без такого согласования.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Использовать согласованное writer/fill publication правило с recheck текущего dirty ownership; копирование одного только sibling get guard без общего race proof недостаточно.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Остановить get_many после inner miss, полностью опубликовать set(K,new), продолжить stale fill, затем многократно wrapper get/get_many и eviction; требовать точные new bytes.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_membuffer.rs:1246](../../../../crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_membuffer.rs); [crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_membuffer.rs:1258](../../../../crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_membuffer.rs); [crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_membuffer.rs:1231](../../../../crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_membuffer.rs); [crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_membuffer.rs:790](../../../../crates/shamir-storage/src/storage_membuffer.rs).

### RV-TRANSPORT-IPC-3C21F8FA — Unix `bind()`: `set_permissions` failure leaks the socket file — with default (wide) permissions — and poisons the next start

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-ipc`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.2](shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md#review-1-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-transport-ipc P0.3 (P0)](shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** When chmod fails after successful Tokio bind, Self is never constructed, so its pathname-removing Drop cannot run. Closing the local socket does not unlink the pathname. Initial permissions depend on umask; a persistent live-channel bypass is not established. The oracle must force chmod failure, inspect owned residue and verify safe rebinding after cleanup.

<a id="rv-transport-ipc-eff55124"></a>

### RV-TRANSPORT-IPC-EFF55124 — Cancelling Windows accept poisons a reusable listener without any I/O error

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-ipc`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P1. Исходные пункты: [shamir-transport-ipc P0.1 (P0)](shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1); [shamir-transport-ipc P1.7 (P1)](shamir-transport-ipc/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-7).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Poll accept while no client is connected: next.take() moves the instance into the future and connect suspends. Dropping that future drops pending without restoring next; a subsequent accept panics. Tokio 1.49.0 published NamedPipeServer::connect is cancellation-safe only while its server object is retained, which this wrapper fails to do. The shipped shutdown branch exits and discards the listener, so ordinary shutdown does not establish production reuse reachability. A cancellation-then-successful-accept oracle is required. This is an additional trigger of the existing ownership root, not a second independent error-state defect.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Resource-error reachability is conditional, not a 255-client witness.

### RV-TRANSPORT-TCP-3A576732 — Zero-length payload write silently emits the close sentinel; module doc contradicts spec §2

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `low`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-tcp`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-transport-tcp/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 1](shamir-transport-tcp/api-wire-protocol.md#review-1); [shamir-transport-tcp/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-transport-tcp/correctness-tdd.md#review-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** write_frame([]), write_frame_into([]), and a four-zero-byte prereserved buffer emit close; readers cannot return empty data. The false empty-data documentation remains. Current msgpack callers produce nonempty payloads; exclusive use of write_close is not itself a protocol requirement.

- **Counter-evidence / границы:** Empty writes produce a valid close marker rather than empty data.

### RV-TRANSPORT-TCP-7DFC88FF — `Box<dyn Error + Send + Sync>` in library APIs instead of a `thiserror` enum

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `nit`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-tcp`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.2](shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md#review-6-2); [shamir-transport-tcp/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 2](shamir-transport-tcp/api-wire-protocol.md#review-2); [shamir-transport-tcp/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 8](shamir-transport-tcp/correctness-tdd.md#review-8); [shamir-transport-tcp/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 2](shamir-transport-tcp/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-2); [shamir-transport-tcp/security-crypto.md · Claim 4](shamir-transport-tcp/security-crypto.md#review-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Both TLS constructors retain boxed errors and a string missing-key case. Concrete causes can be downcast; server stringification is its own choice. An enumerable typed surface improves diagnostics/convention conformance, but boxing at this boundary is not a medium runtime defect.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Typed enumeration is absent, but concrete errors can be downcast and callers need not string-match. Typed variants improve contract assertions, but boxing does not prevent programmatic cause inspection. Downcasting remains possible; typed variants are a diagnostic/API improvement rather than demonstrated medium runtime harm. They are diagnostic/style limitations, not a demonstrated crypto failure, and concrete errors permit downcasting.

<a id="rv-transport-tcp-0a23323d"></a>

### RV-TRANSPORT-TCP-0A23323D — TLS convenience builders retain conditional provider/suite panic paths; stock auto-install is counter-evidence

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `low`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-tcp`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.3](shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md#review-6-3); [shamir-transport-tcp/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 6](shamir-transport-tcp/correctness-tdd.md#review-6); [shamir-transport-tcp/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-transport-tcp/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-transport-tcp P0.3 (P0)](shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** rustls 0.23.37 auto-installs the unambiguous AWS-LC default. Its crypto/mod.rs and client/client_conn.rs retain panic paths for ambiguous/custom-provider selection without installation or incompatible provider suites. No current-default boot failure is established.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Default features select AWS-LC, so forgetting install_default alone does not cause the claimed panic. Automatic default installation contradicts a universal missing-installation scenario; current defaults do not establish a boot failure.

<a id="rv-transport-tcp-b5244fa2"></a>

### RV-TRANSPORT-TCP-B5244FA2 — Production server can retain an unauthenticated connection indefinitely after sending challenge

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-server`, `shamir-transport-tcp`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-server/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-server/SUMMARY.md#new-1); [shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-transport-tcp/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Прямого явного plan item для собственного root нет; это распределение текущего не-P решения, а не новая исходная маркировка.

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Полная unauthenticated handshake стадия имеет конечный absolute lifetime до admission release.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** A peer completes TLS, sends a valid auth_init within the first-frame timeout, receives challenge, then sends no client_proof or stalls mid-proof. run_handshake awaits the transport read without a timeout; its caller awaits it directly. The local ServerHandshake is not covered by SessionStore GC, and request_loop's idle timer has not started. Global/per-IP caps and rate limiting bound admission but do not reclaim these occupied slots. This contradicts AUTH_PROTOCOL §8.2's HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT lifecycle. Existing slow_loris.rs covers silence before auth_init only. A discriminating oracle must reach challenge, withhold/trickle proof, and observe terminal closure plus slot release under the separate handshake budget.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Current risk is high for indefinite unauthenticated admission-slot occupation; the new TCP report restates this existing root rather than adding another unique defect.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** AuthInit timeout, TLS timeout и count/rate caps есть; proof read и accepted-task shutdown lifetime ими не ограничены.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Один terminal whole-handshake/proof budget, не resettable per-byte idle timer; cancellation/connection ownership должны также работать при ServerHandle shutdown.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Настоящий TCP/WS handshake дойти до challenge, withheld/trickled proof; по deadline peer закрыт, admission slot released. Pre-AuthInit silence тест не проходит нужный seam.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:291](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:718](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:771](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/server/server_launcher.rs:1137](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/server/server_launcher.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/server/server_handle.rs:104](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/server/server_handle.rs); [docs/guide-docs/client-server-protocol-spec/AUTH_PROTOCOL.md:507](../../../guide-docs/client-server-protocol-spec/AUTH_PROTOCOL.md); [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:717](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/server/server_launcher.rs:1165](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/server/server_launcher.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/connection_context.rs:55](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/connection_context.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/scheduler.rs:194](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/scheduler.rs); [crates/shamir-server/tests/slow_loris.rs:79](../../../../crates/shamir-server/tests/slow_loris.rs).

- **Прочитанные original source line fragments (indent stripped; separator /):** [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:291](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs): <code>.read_frame_into(MAX_PRE_AUTH_FRAME, &amp;mut proof_frame_buf) / .await / .is_err()</code>; [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:718](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs): <code>match tokio::time::timeout(ctx.auth_init_timeout, read_fut).await { / Ok(Ok(())) =&gt; {} / Ok(Err(e)) =&gt; {</code>.

<a id="rv-transport-ws-f54426dc"></a>

### RV-TRANSPORT-WS-F54426DC — Native WS acceptance ignores supplied Origin despite the endpoint policy

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-ws`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-transport-ws/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.2](shamir-transport-ws/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-transport-ws P0.3 (P0)](shamir-transport-ws/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** A syntactically valid upgrade to /shamir/v1 containing Origin:https://evil.example passes the native callback because only the path is checked. TRANSPORT_WS §9.2 requires a supplied Origin to match allowed_origins. The native accept API cannot receive that policy, and production supplies none. This is a pre-authentication policy omission, not a demonstrated exporter-authentication or cookie bypass. Oracle: absent native Origin succeeds, supplied allowed Origin succeeds, supplied denied Origin rejects before 101.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/server.rs:78](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/server.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/server.rs:88](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/server.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/server/server_launcher.rs:1394](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/server/server_launcher.rs); [docs/guide-docs/client-server-protocol-spec/TRANSPORT_WS.md:96](../../../guide-docs/client-server-protocol-spec/TRANSPORT_WS.md).

### RV-TRANSPORT-WS-C0C43A18 — Spec-mandated WebSocket subprotocol negotiation is unimplemented

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-ws`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-transport-ws/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.1](shamir-transport-ws/SUMMARY.md#review-5-1); [shamir-transport-ws/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 1](shamir-transport-ws/api-wire-protocol.md#review-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Both callbacks omit selection. A browser offering shamir-v1 receives no acknowledgement and must fail under WHATWG §2.2; first-party adapters avoid this only by offering nothing. Application V1 checking does not repair negotiation. Oracle: offered-v1 must succeed with the selected header; incompatible offers must receive 400.

<a id="rv-tx-1c75483a"></a>

### RV-TX-1C75483A — One minimum journal-gap marker hides later known overflow losses

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-tx`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.4](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-4).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-tx P0.4 (P0)](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Запрос suffix должен сообщать любой известный loss внутри suffix, даже если самый ранний gap расположен раньше start.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Overflow drops at versions 10 and 20 leave first_gap_version = 10. read_from(11, ...) returns gap_at None because 10 < 11, even though the requested suffix contains the known missing event 20. This needs neither restart nor corrupt storage and contradicts the any-known-dropped-version suffix promise. The CF-1 test currently requires None for requests past the minimum, without proving there were no later drops. Oracle: force two loss points and query between them; require a loss/resync signal for the later hole, using sparse event semantics rather than numeric continuity.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Сигнал первого gap есть, но единственный minimum теряет сведения о последующих drops.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Хранить достаточную историю/диапазоны loss и корректно проецировать её на suffix; не подменять sparse commit semantics обязательной плотной numeric continuity.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Создать известные drops 10 и 20, запросить from=11, потребовать loss/resync сигнал для 20; requests past first_gap без второго gap не являются отличающим oracle.

<a id="rv-tx-ae700bbd"></a>

### RV-TX-AE700BBD — Durable journal events have no schema/version envelope; decode failures are silently skipped

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-tx`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.6](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#review-3-6); [shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.1](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#review-5-1); [shamir-tx/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 1](shamir-tx/api-wire-protocol.md#review-1); [shamir-tx/security-crypto.md · Claim 1](shamir-tx/security-crypto.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-tx P0.4 (P0)](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-4).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** An undecodable stored event is omitted without an error/gap result. No explicit format envelope exists, but absence alone is prospective compatibility debt. Exact rmp-serde 1.3.1 to_vec encodes structs positionally as arrays, so ordinary field renaming does not change those bytes. Published source: https://docs.rs/crate/rmp-serde/1.3.1/source/src/encode.rs

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Decoder failure only warns and does not affect the returned error/gap state. Malformed stored payloads trigger a warning but disappear from JournalRead without an error or gap signal.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs:411](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs:416](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs:543](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs); [Cargo.lock:2949](../../../../Cargo.lock); [crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs:87](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs:409](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/changefeed.rs).

### RV-TX-D6B83C2B — CF-1 gap signal is volatile; read_from never checks contiguity

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-tx`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.3](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#review-5-3); [shamir-tx/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 3](shamir-tx/api-wire-protocol.md#review-3).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Overflow evidence resets on construction and is not durable. Restart can therefore erase known event-loss evidence. Commit versions legitimately skip aborted, index-only, or per-record batch allocations; dense-version checking is not a valid repair. Crash-tail loss itself is explicitly documented as a trade-off.

<a id="rv-tx-360d839c"></a>

### RV-TX-360D839C — finalize_reservation is not max-monotonic — the ack-path publish can regress a cell below a newer committed version

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-tx`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-tx/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-tx/correctness-tdd.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-tx P0.2 (P0)](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Ack-path cell publication max-monotone при out-of-order Snapshot commits, с корректным ownership clearing reservation.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Plain Snapshot writers need not hold commit_lock. Publishing key K at v11 and then v10 overwrites its cell with 10; exact-version current reads can return the older value. Existing monotonic tests invoke publish_cell or seed_version, not this publisher. An oracle must invoke apply_committed_visible in reverse version order and assert both cell version and returned bytes.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** publish_cell и seed_version уже сравнивают version; finalize_reservation plain assignment остаётся.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Сохранить больший cell version и очищать reservation по ownership-протоколу; проверять именно finalize_reservation/apply_committed_visible, не только уже monotone seed_version.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Опубликовать apply_committed_visible K@11, затем K@10; проверить cell version=11 и bytes версии 11, включая two-writer interleaving.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs:668](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/tx/commit.rs:921](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/tx/commit.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs:664](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mvcc_history.rs:521](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mvcc_history.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/tests/mvcc_store_tests/publish_monotonic_tests.rs:47](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/tests/mvcc_store_tests/publish_monotonic_tests.rs).

<a id="rv-tx-2cb455a7"></a>

### RV-TX-2CB455A7 — Failed history.transact leaves RecordCell advanced with no rollback — prior version masked on point reads

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-tx`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.1](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#review-6-1); [shamir-tx/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-tx/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-tx P0.1 (P0)](shamir-tx/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Failed direct history write не должен маскировать уже существующее committed значение или перетирать более нового успешного писателя.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** With committed k::5 present, a failed write at 6 bumps the cell first; guard Drop marks 6 Aborted and advances the floor but does not restore the cell. Exact k::6 lookup returns None although k::5 remains. Direct engine CRUD callers exist. Oracle must start with an existing value and assert exact point/stream preservation after failure, including a later-operation batch fault.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** VersionGuard Drop отмечает Aborted/прогресс, не восстанавливает RecordCell; внешняя history_of overlay правка не исправляет эти point reads.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Проектировать ownership-aware publication/failure reconciliation с учётом нескольких pending writes и partial Store::transact; unconditional restore(old_v) и простая задержка publication недостаточны.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Сначала k::5 со строго заданными bytes; fault записи k::6 и позднего batch op; point/stream должны сохранить k::5. Второй сценарий допускает успешного concurrent writer и запрещает его откат старым failure.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs:785](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs:799](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs:1294](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs:1291](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/mvcc_store/mod.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/version_guard.rs:107](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/version_guard.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/table/table_manager_crud.rs:218](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/table/table_manager_crud.rs).

### RV-TX-3AA40F9C — Changefeed journal gap detection has an undetected hole on persist failures

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-tx`.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Failed serialization or put only warns; the writer has no gap-marker handle. Closed sends are ignored. A later successful higher-version put advances max persisted past the missing event. A failing-put then successful-put oracle must assert an explicit loss/error signal, not just maximum progress.

<a id="rv-types-2a1ab215"></a>

### RV-TYPES-2A1AB215 — Hydrated tuple count is misused as a durable interner ID cursor

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-types`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-types/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.2](shamir-types/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Прямого явного plan item для собственного root нет; это распределение текущего не-P решения, а не новая исходная маркировка.

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Durable interner progress — ID high-water с учётом holes/repeated tuples, не число hydrated tuples.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** A legitimate gap can make entries_after repeatedly persist mappings above it. For durable chunks [(1,a),(3,c)], [(3,c)], [(3,c)], hydration has four tuples but maximum ID 3; InternerManager seeds last_persisted_len to 4. A new name receives ID 4, yet persist scans only IDs greater than 4 and returns without saving it. InternerTouch can therefore acknowledge a supposedly durable mapping that is absent on reopen, and persisted_high_water already falsely claims ID 4. Permanent gaps can arise from normal raced touch_ind reservations. Existing gap tests do not reopen a manager after duplicate recapture; the needed oracle follows recapture, reopen, new touch, persist, and a second reopen.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Publication allocation holes реальны; hydration задаёт count=4 при maxID=3 и преждевременно объявляет id4 persisted.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Восстанавливать ID-based cursor и не пропускать newly allocated mapping; согласовать entries_after/last_persisted_len/persisted_high_water и A8 semantics, не запрещать легальные recapture duplicates только ради счётчика.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Chunks [(1,a),(3,c)], [(3,c)], [(3,c)] → reopen → allocate id4 → persist → second reopen должен разрешать id4. One-process gap test этого не видит.

<a id="rv-types-6866d8b8"></a>

### RV-TYPES-6866D8B8 — Ext32 skip arithmetic violates the lens's panic-free promise on 32-bit targets

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `low`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-types`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-types/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.4](shamir-types/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-4).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-types P0.1 (P0)](shamir-types/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** The Ext32 skip arm reads n as usize and computes 1+n before passing it to checked_add. With n=u32::MAX on a 32-bit target, that intermediate addition panics with overflow checks or wraps without them. A tiny map containing this value before a searched-for field reaches the arm; no huge allocation is required. The matched-value decoder uses separate checked addition and does not have this particular bug. Current native 64-bit deployment is not the witness; a target-gated malformed Ext32 skip oracle is needed.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs:401](../../../../crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs); [crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs:404](../../../../crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs); [crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs:598](../../../../crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs); [crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs:849](../../../../crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs); [crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/mod.rs:38](../../../../crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/mod.rs).

- **Прочитанные original source line fragments (indent stripped; separator /):** [crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs:401](../../../../crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs): <code>0xc9 =&gt; { / let n = read_be::&lt;4&gt;(buf, pos)? as usize; / *pos = pos</code>; [crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs:404](../../../../crates/shamir-types/src/record_view/lens.rs): <code>.checked_add(1 + n) / .ok_or(RecordViewError::Truncated(start))?; / Ok(())</code>.

### RV-TYPES-BB78489C — Unbounded preallocation from attacker-controlled msgpack headers -- allocator abort, unlike the capped tree visitor

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-types`.

<a id="rv-wal-a025886e"></a>

### RV-WAL-A025886E — The claimed stale-sidecar fix remains unsafe when sidecar removal fails

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** memory/ownership safety — scoped reachability. **Модули:** `shamir-wal`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md#new-1); [shamir-wal/correctness-tdd.md · Claim Notes/stale-sidecar-handling](shamir-wal/correctness-tdd.md#review-notes-stale-sidecar-handling).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Прямого явного plan item для собственного root нет; это распределение текущего не-P решения, а не новая исходная маркировка.

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Stale sidecar не может определять future truncation после неуспешной invalidation и новых WAL appends.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** A reactivated segment has a readable CRC-valid meta maximum 10. Unlink failure is warned and swallowed; appending version 60 is allowed. If poison rotation also cannot remove that stale sidecar, a later open trusts 10 and truncate_below(55) deletes version-60 data. A concrete precondition is a sidecar that remains readable but cannot be removed/replaced while WAL and directory operations remain allowed. Current tests force successful removal only. Invalidation failure must prevent stale future trust, not merely clear an in-memory flag.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Neither test forces unlink failure, which is swallowed and permits subsequent data loss.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Обе успешные invalidation ветки существуют и покрыты assertions; failure намеренно предупреждается и допускает дальнейшие writes.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Сделать invalidation failure явным препятствием unsafe reuse/trust и учитывать restart state; warning не устраняет читаемый stale максимум.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Readable valid meta max10, unlink/replace failure при разрешённых WAL/dir ops, append60, poison rotation, reopen, truncate55 — версия60 не удаляется; успешный unlink-only тест не различает failure.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs:187](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs:307](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_meta.rs:164](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_meta.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs:145](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs:471](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/tests/segment_set_tests.rs:890](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/tests/segment_set_tests.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/tests/segment_set_tests.rs:791](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/tests/segment_set_tests.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/tests/segment_set_tests.rs:924](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/tests/segment_set_tests.rs).

<a id="rv-wal-7da7b0a9"></a>

### RV-WAL-7DA7B0A9 — Circuit breaker can strand parked appenders indefinitely (L1 violation, hang)

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-wal`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md · Claim 1.1](shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md#review-1-1); [shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-wal/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 1](shamir-wal/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-1); [shamir-wal/correctness-tdd.md · Claim 1](shamir-wal/correctness-tdd.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-wal P0.1 (P0)](shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-1).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Каждый accepted group-commit waiter в taken или pending window достигает terminal outcome даже при circuit-breaker exit.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Leader takes A; B queues during a failing sink await and loses election. The breaker completes only A and clears flushing; B only awaits its Notify. With no new append, B never resolves. A discriminating test must pause the leader before B queues, then fail and release it.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Followers admitted after mem::take fail their only CAS and remain uncompleted when the failing leader returns.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Taken waiters могут завершаться false, но более поздние pending waiters не получают терминального результата; helper enable-before-check не создаёт producer события.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Под pending admission протоколом settle весь оставшийся queue либо передать доказанному живому owner; не просто очищать flushing. Сохранить requested durability tier и honest error outcome.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Leader takes batch, sink repeatedly fails, follower admitted после take и до breaker return; без следующего unrelated append follower должен завершиться ошибкой/defined handoff, не висеть. Background sync-only tick не считается owner pending commits.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs:327](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs:196](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs:277](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs:189](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs).

- **Прочитанные original source line fragments (indent stripped; separator /):** [crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs:327](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs): <code>if !write_ok || (needs_fsync &amp;&amp; !sync_ok) { / self.flushing.store(false, Ordering::Release); / return;</code>; [crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs:196](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs): <code>notified.await; / if waiter.ok.load(Ordering::Acquire) { / Ok(())</code>.

<a id="rv-wal-81969ddb"></a>

### RV-WAL-81969DDB — Cancellation or panic of the leader task wedges flushing forever — permanent WAL append hang

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-wal`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.1](shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md#review-6-1); [shamir-wal/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 1](shamir-wal/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-1).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-wal P0.2 (P0)](shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-2).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Drop elected append future не оставляет permanent leadership latch или забытые taken waiters, и не теряет ownership продолжающейся blocking I/O.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Drop an elected append future while awaiting File work: neither normal release executes, flushing remains true and taken waiters disappear from pending without completion. 09d2e215 detached a different engine GroupCommit; cooperative server deadlines are counter-evidence to the claimed ordinary remote-timeout trigger.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** 09d2e215 закрывал engine GroupCommit, не этот WAL flushing latch. Cooperative request deadline — counter к обычному timeout trigger, не proof общей cancel safety.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Владеть coordinator work/всеми waiters независимо от отменяемого caller, либо иметь полный cancellation protocol. Простого RAII clearing flag недостаточно: spawn_blocking работа уже может продолжаться и новый leader не должен нарушать single-appender/file rollback ordering.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Остановить настоящее File append/sync blocking work, отменить leader future после election/take, затем followers/new append; проверить все outcomes, ровный replay и дальнейший прогресс. Engine GroupCommit detached-leader fixture относится к другому coordinator.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs:185](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/wal_group_commit.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/repo/group_commit/group_commit.rs:74](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/repo/group_commit/group_commit.rs); [crates/shamir-tx/src/repo_wal_manager.rs:91](../../../../crates/shamir-tx/src/repo_wal_manager.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/query/batch/batch_execute.rs:779](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/query/batch/batch_execute.rs).

<a id="rv-wal-3b29232e"></a>

### RV-WAL-3B29232E — Seal-time fsync failure fails an already-successful append whose frames survive — acked-failed tx resurrected on replay

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-wal`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md · Claim 6.2](shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md#review-6-2); [shamir-wal/error-handling-lifecycle.md · Claim 2](shamir-wal/error-handling-lifecycle.md#review-2).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0. Исходные пункты: [shamir-wal P0.3 (P0)](shamir-wal/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Engine не объявляет definite pre-commit abort там, где physical WAL frames уже написаны и могут replay; Synced acknowledgement не выдаётся без его durability.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** A successful physical append crosses the threshold; seal fsync Err propagates, and the group reports failure without removing the written frames. commit.rs treats WAL failure as pre-commit abort, yet a later recovery can replay those frames. New-active open failure produces the same outcome even after successful old-segment fsync.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Rotation error по-прежнему propagates после append success; commit caller трактует такой Err как до-commit abort, а written frames могут survive.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Различить append persistence outcome от housekeeping seal/rotation failure и terminal unknown outcome; согласовать commit/version tracker/recovery. Нельзя просто warn-and-Ok после failed sync для Synced tier или обещать rollback, не удалив written frames безопасно.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Настоящий append завершается записью, затем forced seal fsync/new-active open fault; сопоставить returned outcome, live aborted/materialized tracker, WAL replay после reopen. Требовать согласованный defined outcome, не resurrected definite-abort.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs:251](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs:390](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/src/tx/commit.rs:977](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/src/tx/commit.rs); [crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs:367](../../../../crates/shamir-wal/src/segment_set.rs).

### RV-WASM-HOST-4C69CFE0 — HTTP wire codec collapses duplicate headers (`Set-Cookie` loss on both directions)

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-sdk`, `shamir-wasm-host`.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Two identically named Set-Cookie response entries overwrite in host encoding. Two identically named request entries survive SDK serialization but overwrite in QueryValue decoding. Requires configured egress; this proves silent multiplicity loss, not an authentication compromise.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Two identical header keys are serialized by the SDK, then host map insertion retains only the last value; response insertion does likewise. Workspace e2e exists but lacks duplicate/malformed oracles.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** Dropping a child call during an await bypasses its final fetch_sub. A top-level timeout can do this while the parent still charges its own Store. <a id="rv-wasm-host-b1a78b5b"></a>

### RV-WASM-HOST-B1A78B5B — Reserved colon-prefixed map keys violate guest-to-host value identity

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `medium`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-sdk`, `shamir-types`, `shamir-wasm-host`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.1](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-1); [shamir-types/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.5](shamir-types/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-5); [shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.3](shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-3).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P1. Исходные пункты: [shamir-sdk P1.5 (P1)](shamir-sdk/SUMMARY.md#plan-p1-5); [shamir-types P0.3 (P0)](shamir-types/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3); [shamir-wasm-host P0.5 (P0)](shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-5).

- **Нарушенный invariant / проверяемая обязанность:** Обычный production QueryValue/SDK Map roundtrip сохраняет literal field names; legacy typed prefix не меняет их без явной границы.

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** SDK Value::Map serializes literal keys, but host QueryValue is the same Value<String> decoder used for prefixed fixtures. A guest map containing i:count with Int(7) becomes count with Int(7); including both names can overwrite a field, while a noninteger i:count can reject an otherwise supported SDK Map. The defect reaches returned values, nested-call parameters, globals and DB documents. rmp-serde 1.3.1 dispatches to that custom map visitor; its depth guard does not repair key semantics. Exact archive source: [rmp-serde 1.3.1 decode.rs](https://docs.rs/crate/rmp-serde/1.3.1/source/src/decode.rs). Existing SDK conformance fixtures use ordinary keys and cannot catch this; test literal reserved-prefix keys against the unchanged-key contract.

- **Дополнительные counter-evidence / границы:** Encoding a normal map with key i:age and integer 7 writes that exact key, but decoding returns age; keys i:age and age can collapse, and a noninteger value under i:age is rejected. Existing u-prefix tests intentionally pin legacy interpretation but do not test ordinary field-name preservation.

- **Что source-fixed и что остаётся:** Существующие u-prefix fixtures намеренно пинят legacy parsing, не доказывают ordinary-name identity; совместный host/SDK root дедуплицирован.

- **Safe direction (без реализации):** Разделить legacy parser boundary от обычного QueryValue decode и согласовать SDK/host/network consumers; aliases UserValue/QueryValue нельзя различать TypeId. Обещанный legacy policy менять явно.

- **Различающий future oracle (не написан и не запускался):** Map i:age=7 и age=8, отдельно i:age со строкой, nested maps и unknown xml:lang; real host/wire decoders должны сохранять literal names либо явно reject по принятому контракту, не silently collapse.

<a id="rv-wasm-host-d2732518"></a>

### RV-WASM-HOST-D2732518 — The delegated Argon2 concurrency semaphore can lose its final wakeup

**Verdict:** `confirmed-open`; **scoped risk:** `high`; **тип:** scoped correctness/lifecycle/API. **Модули:** `shamir-funclib`, `shamir-wasm-host`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.2](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-2-2); [shamir-funclib/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 2](shamir-funclib/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-2); [shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.4](shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-4).

**P provenance:** Минимальный исходный P среди явно связанных plan items/связанных NEW: P0, P2. Исходные пункты: [shamir-funclib P0.3 (P0)](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#plan-p0-3); [shamir-wasm-host P2.28 (P2)](shamir-wasm-host/SUMMARY.md#plan-p2-28).

- **Точный trigger, consequence и reachability:** At saturation, a waiter holds notify's mutex, observes available=0 at the while predicate, and is descheduled before Condvar::wait. Hash holders can release all permits and notify without acquiring that mutex. Notifications occur before the waiter registers and are lost; it can then sleep with permits available and no remaining releaser. This is a reachable blocking-pool liveness defect, not failure of the 16-hash cap. [Pinned Rust 1.94.0 Condvar source](https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/blob/1.94.0/library/std/src/sync/poison/condvar.rs) confirms notifications are not buffered. The existing barrier/KDF-duration test cannot force this interval; a deterministic check-to-wait schedule or model is the discriminating oracle. Commit 190e1f08's no-lost-wakeup assurance is contradicted by this witness.

- **Current file:line evidence:** [crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs:140](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs); [crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs:145](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs); [crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs:139](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs); [crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto/tests/crypto_tests.rs:207](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto/tests/crypto_tests.rs); [rust-toolchain.toml:15](../../../../rust-toolchain.toml); [crates/shamir-wasm-host/src/builtin.rs:63](../../../../crates/shamir-wasm-host/src/builtin.rs); [crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs:221](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs); [crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto/tests/crypto_tests.rs:204](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto/tests/crypto_tests.rs).

- **Прочитанные original source line fragments (indent stripped; separator /):** [crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs:140](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs): <code>while !try_take(&amp;self.available) { / guard = cvar.wait(guard).expect("semaphore condvar poisoned"); / fn release(&amp;self) {</code>; [crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs:145](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/crypto.rs): <code>fn release(&amp;self) { / self.available.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Release); / self.notify.1.notify_one();</code>.

**Verdict:** `refuted`; **scoped risk:** `—`; **тип:** closed/calibration. **Модули:** `shamir-connect`.

**Происхождение:** [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim Executive summary](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-executive-summary); [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Claim 2.1](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#review-2-1); [shamir-connect/concurrency-lockfree.md · Claim 1](shamir-connect/concurrency-lockfree.md#review-1).

- **Положительное основание закрытия / scope:** For identical advanced timestamps, one atomic fetch_max receives the old value; subsequent operations receive the advanced value. Positive intervals telescope even with out-of-order timestamps. Retried token closures do not publish repeated refill.

<a id="original-plan-items"></a>

## Все исходные plan items этого P

Вердикт относится к actual scoped obligation, не к обещанию реализовать historical recipe. Комбинации направлены к нескольким независимым cards; redirects сохраняют собственный P. Рецепты с unconditional rollback, dense numeric contiguity, identity-changing normalization, blanket WHERE/envelope/versioning или unboxed required host futures не принимаются автоматически.

Public Sq8Quantizer approx_dot/approx_l2_sq/dequant_norm_sq принимают лишь &[u8], проверяют length, не alignment; safe offset slice может попасть в три NEON kernels при n≥4. Это confirmed conditional soundness дефект, не наблюдённый crash/exploit и не blanket unsafe certification. |
| [shamir-index/security-crypto.md · Claim 7](shamir-index/security-crypto.md#review-7) | `unverified` → `confirmed-open` | Перепроверка current declared Rust 1.95.0: exact generated.rs:16503–16506 vld1_lane_u32 использует simd_insert!(src, LANE as u32, *ptr), т.е. typed u32 dereference с alignment requirement. Public Sq8Quantizer approx_dot/approx_l2_sq/dequant_norm_sq принимают лишь &[u8], проверяют length, не alignment; safe offset slice может попасть в три NEON kernels при n≥4. Это confirmed conditional soundness дефект, не наблюдённый crash/exploit и не blanket unsafe certification. |

Внешний **SLICE-TX-HISTORY** source fix уже существовал в current tree: retained overlay timeline объединён с history, new tests зарегистрированы. Это не Rust fix этого обзора, не passing run и не полная multi-reader/retirement гарантия — [P1 slice](P1.md#slice-tx-history).

Changed pins перепроверены: Wasmtime 48.0.5 сохраняет mixed-import getter failure; rustls 0.23.45 сохраняет auto-install counter-evidence/conditional provider границы; Rust 1.95 source contracts не заменяют compiler runs. Исправлены четыре унаследованные out-of-file source citations с фактически прочитанными current lines. Все 94 NEW/non-summary observations и 24 не-P Plan IDs учтены; исторические “unique defects”, severity totals и timing percentages не приняты за текущие доказательства.


**Новые не-input source observations xxs:** [XXS-NEW-TX-PREFIX](P2.md#xxs-tx-prefix-vacuum) (scan orig discarded; data protected только пока overlay retained) и [XXS-NEW-TX-TIMESTAMP](P1.md#xxs-tx-shared-timestamp) (shared ts removed per-key, visible после legitimate pending-stamp GC). Это 2 independent mechanisms, не +2 к 94 existing observation rows и не одна umbrella vacuum проблема. Fixed-length engine keys и same-table shared-version conditions сохранены.

## Root/context и ancillary coverage

Root SUMMARY/CRATE_PRIORITY полностью прочитаны, включая history; ranking/roadmap повторяет module claims/plans/NEW и не добавляет IDs. В каждом из 187 module документов sections Scope, Guarantee checks, Evidence/recipe corrections, reviewer comparison и prior history прочитаны; они уточняют ниже трассированные ID. Supported calibration statements без самостоятельного finding не превращены в дополнительные bugs. Исторические рецепты сохранены лишь как provenance; текущие безопасные ограничения находятся в карточках/plan table.

## Статическая приёмка и предел доказательств

Контроль относится только к Markdown, inventory арифметике и сохранности прочитанного source slice. Проверяемые predicates: 189 input links/fingerprints; 3176 distinct current `(path,kind,id)` coverage assignments; все 447 plan priorities, включая 24 не-P IDs; valid relative targets/explicit anchors; complete card fields без незаполненных мест; ровно три разрешённых repository writes. Вычисленные counts приведены выше. Никаких compiler/build/lint/test/benchmark/run outcomes этот обзор не заявляет; future oracles — требования следующей отдельно разрешённой работы. Post-write readback/static validation outcome передаётся родителю отдельно, не подменяет непроверенные runtime premises.
